    "prompt": "You are working on the project \"Grain to Pixel\" in the repository at the current working directory. Follow docs/workflow.md: read this issue, post a short proposal (understanding, approach, files, tests, docs, risks, open questions) and STOP for my explicit approval before writing code. After approval implement with tests and docs, run the CI gates from docs/workflow.md section 8, open a PR that closes the GitHub issue, then report and ask for the next step.\n\nPROJECT CONTEXT (from docs/product/idea.md)\n- Grain to Pixel is a personal Windows 10/11 and macOS desktop app for close friends only, no monetization: an all-in-one tool to scan, convert, edit and organize film negatives (commercial licences are prohibitive).\n- Core workflow: ingest via Nikon Coolscan LS-50 (RGB + IR; the author's modded scanner scans whole rolls of up to 40 frames) or via separate R/G/B exposures shot with a Nikon D600 (NEF) under a Scanlight V4 light, adding tags -> determine frame area and measure film base -> convert to positive -> edit colour balance, tone, geometry, IR/ICE dust removal -> searchable tagged archive.\n- Non-goals: masking, AI generation, advanced retouching. Success: activation, and results comparable to Nikon Scan 4 / Trichroma Lab. Risk: little colour-negative sample data. Constraint: built with Claude Pro, so keep the session narrow and read only what this issue needs.\n\nTECHNICAL CONTEXT (docs/specification.md, docs/architecture.md, docs/structure.md, docs/convention.md)\n- Stack: Tauri 2; Rust workspace in crates/ (gtp-core, gtp-io, gtp-negative, gtp-edit, gtp-catalog, gtp-ingest, gtp-capture, gtp-app); React 18 + TypeScript (strict) + Vite UI in apps/desktop with Zustand, Radix UI and CSS modules; generated packages/ipc-types (tauri-specta); SQLite catalog (rusqlite bundled, FTS5, forward-only migrations); tracing logs; i18n via t() with en.json only.\n- Architecture: feature crates depend on gtp-core (gtp-ingest also on gtp-io) and never on each other; gtp-app is the application layer that combines them (services, JobManager, pipeline); src-tauri is a thin adapter (validate, call a gtp-app service, map errors to AppError); the UI never touches pixels and receives rendered bytes. Long work runs as cancellable jobs with progress events. Hardware sits behind traits (ScannerSource, LightSource, CameraSource) with fakes.\n- Pipeline (spec 4.2) fixed order: load -> IR dust -> crop -> film-base normalize -> invert -> colour -> tone -> geometry -> output transform; internal f32 linear with Rec.2020 primaries; originals immutable, all edits in the versioned Recipe.\n- Repo layout: apps/desktop (src React UI, src-tauri shell), crates/*, packages/{ipc-types,ui}, tools/{benchmark,scripts}, samples/{public,private}, docs/, decisions/ (ADRs), rules/, prompts/.\n- Conventions: no unwrap/expect/unsafe/println in committed Rust, no console.log or any in TS; validate at boundaries; AppError codes; tracing spans; tests are mandatory (unit, golden, fakes, component); Conventional Commits; performance budgets in spec section 11; security in spec section 9.\n\nISSUE D-04 – Capture commands, profile persistence and light safety hooks\nMilestone: M6 Guided RGB capture | Area: Desktop shell (Tauri) | Type: feature | Size: M (1d)\nSpec references: specification sections 6 and 6.1\nDependencies (must be Done first; if any is not, stop and tell me): H-08 – Capture orchestrator, CameraSource, FakeCamera and safety; L-03 – Library create/open, folder layout and settings\n\nDESCRIPTION\nExpose capture to the UI: light and camera commands, test shot, profile persistence, events, and light-off on app exit.\n\nTECHNICAL DETAILS\nFILES: apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/capture.rs; exit hook in lib.rs; events `capture://step {channel, state: lit|fired|received}`, `light://status {temp_c, vbus_mv}`.\nROUTES: `light_list() -> PortInfo[]`, `light_connect(port?) -> LightInfo`, `light_set([u8;3])`, `light_off()`, `camera_list() -> CameraInfo[]`, `capture_profile_get() -> CaptureProfile`, `capture_profile_set(patch)`, `capture_test(Channel) -> TestShot { histogram, clipped_pct }`, `capture_triplet(RollId) -> JobId`.\nMIGRATIONS: none.\nTESTS: command layer with fakes, exit hook turns the light off, validation.\n\nACCEPTANCE CRITERIA\n- All capture commands work against fakes (`GTP_FAKE_HW=1`) with regenerated ipc-types\n- The light is switched off on app exit, window close and view unmount\n- Test shot returns histogram and clipped percentage; `capture://step` and `light://status` events are emitted\n- Profile is validated (levels 0..=255, warn < abort) and persisted in settings.json\n\nNeeds from the human: approval of your proposal.\nDefinition of Done: docs/workflow.md section 11. Update the docs this change makes stale. Do not widen scope; propose follow-up issues instead.",
    "body": "## Description\nExpose capture to the UI: light and camera commands, test shot, profile persistence, events, and light-off on app exit.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n- [ ] All capture commands work against fakes (`GTP_FAKE_HW=1`) with regenerated ipc-types\n- [ ] The light is switched off on app exit, window close and view unmount\n- [ ] Test shot returns histogram and clipped percentage; `capture://step` and `light://status` events are emitted\n- [ ] Profile is validated (levels 0..=255, warn < abort) and persisted in settings.json\n\n## Estimate\n1d (size M)\n\n## Dependencies\n- {{dep:H-08}} H-08 – Capture orchestrator, CameraSource, FakeCamera and safety\n- {{dep:L-03}} L-03 – Library create/open, folder layout and settings\n\n## References\n- Spec: specification sections 6 and 6.1\n- Milestone: M6 Guided RGB capture; area: Desktop shell (Tauri); previous ID: GTP-084"
  },

Follow docs/workflow.md: read this issue, post a short proposal (understanding, approach, files, tests, docs, risks, open questions) and STOP for my explicit approval before writing code. After approval implement with tests and docs, run the CI gates from docs/workflow.md section 8, open a PR that closes the GitHub issue, then report and ask for the next step.\n\nPROJECT CONTEXT (from docs/product/idea.md)\n- Grain to Pixel is a personal Windows 10/11 and macOS desktop app for close friends only, no monetization: an all-in-one tool to scan, convert, edit and organize film negatives (commercial licences are prohibitive).\n- Core workflow: ingest via Nikon Coolscan LS-50 (RGB + IR; the author's modded scanner scans whole rolls of up to 40 frames) or via separate R/G/B exposures shot with a Nikon D600 (NEF) under a Scanlight V4 light, adding tags -> determine frame area and measure film base -> convert to positive -> edit colour balance, tone, geometry, IR/ICE dust removal -> searchable tagged archive.\n- Non-goals: masking, AI generation, advanced retouching. Success: activation, and results comparable to Nikon Scan 4 / Trichroma Lab. Risk: little colour-negative sample data. Constraint: built with Claude Pro, so keep the session narrow and read only what this issue needs.\n\nTECHNICAL CONTEXT (docs/specification.md, docs/architecture.md, docs/structure.md, docs/convention.md)\n- Stack: Tauri 2; Rust workspace in crates/ (gtp-core, gtp-io, gtp-negative, gtp-edit, gtp-catalog, gtp-ingest, gtp-capture, gtp-app); React 18 + TypeScript (strict) + Vite UI in apps/desktop with Zustand, Radix UI and CSS modules; generated packages/ipc-types (tauri-specta); SQLite catalog (rusqlite bundled, FTS5, forward-only migrations); tracing logs; i18n via t() with en.json only.\n- Architecture: feature crates depend on gtp-core (gtp-ingest also on gtp-io) and never on each other; gtp-app is the application layer that combines them (services, JobManager, pipeline); src-tauri is a thin adapter (validate, call a gtp-app service, map errors to AppError); the UI never touches pixels and receives rendered bytes. Long work runs as cancellable jobs with progress events. Hardware sits behind traits (ScannerSource, LightSource, CameraSource) with fakes.\n- Pipeline (spec 4.2) fixed order: load -> IR dust -> crop -> film-base normalize -> invert -> colour -> tone -> geometry -> output transform; internal f32 linear with Rec.2020 primaries; originals immutable, all edits in the versioned Recipe.\n- Repo layout: apps/desktop (src React UI, src-tauri shell), crates/*, packages/{ipc-types,ui}, tools/{benchmark,scripts}, samples/{public,private}, docs/, decisions/ (ADRs), rules/, prompts/.\n- Conventions: no unwrap/expect/unsafe/println in committed Rust, no console.log or any in TS; validate at boundaries; AppError codes; tracing spans; tests are mandatory (unit, golden, fakes, component); Conventional Commits; performance budgets in spec section 11; security in spec section 9.\n\nISSUE D-05 – Diagnostics export and local counters\nMilestone: M7 MVP release v0.1.0 | Area: Desktop shell (Tauri) | Type: feature | Size: M (1d)\nSpec references: specification sections 12 and 14\nDependencies (must be Done first; if any is not, stop and tell me): D-03 – Logging, panic hook and client error reporting; L-03 – Library create/open, folder layout and settings; P-12 – Export job - TIFF16 and JPEG with ICC\n\nDESCRIPTION\nZip of logs and system info for support, plus local-only activation counters.\n\nTECHNICAL DETAILS\nFILES: crates/gtp-app/src/services/diagnostics.rs; counters stored in the catalog meta table and incremented from import, develop, export and scan services; Settings UI button.\nROUTES: `diagnostics_export(dest: String)`.\nMIGRATIONS: none.\nTESTS: zip contents, counter increments, no pixel data.\n\nACCEPTANCE CRITERIA\n- diagnostics_export zips logs, versions, schema version, flags, counters and last job errors; no images and no full paths at info level\n- Counters first_import_at, first_convert_at, first_export_at, frames_imported, frames_exported, scans_failed increment at the right events, once for first_* values\n- Settings shows counters and an Export diagnostics button\n\nNeeds from the human: approval of your proposal.\nDefinition of Done: docs/workflow.md section 11. Update the docs this change makes stale. Do not widen scope; propose follow-up issues instead.",
    "body": "## Description\nZip of logs and system info for support, plus local-only activation counters.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n- [ ] diagnostics_export zips logs, versions, schema version, flags, counters and last job errors; no images and no full paths at info level\n- [ ] Counters first_import_at, first_convert_at, first_export_at, frames_imported, frames_exported, scans_failed increment at the right events, once for first_* values\n- [ ] Settings shows counters and an Export diagnostics button\n\n## Estimate\n1d (size M)\n\n## Dependencies\n- {{dep:D-03}} D-03 – Logging, panic hook and client error reporting\n- {{dep:L-03}} L-03 – Library create/open, folder layout and settings\n- {{dep:P-12}} P-12 – Export job - TIFF16 and JPEG with ICC\n\n## References\n- Spec: specification sections 12 and 14\n- Milestone: M7 MVP release v0.1.0; area: Desktop shell (Tauri); previous ID: GTP-092"
  },

  {
    "id": "T-07",
    "title": "T-07 – Make the Windows WebDriver e2e run on CI runners",
    "description": "The WebDriver e2e (tauri-driver + msedgedriver) passes locally but cannot start a session on GitHub-hosted Windows runners, so it is a manual/weekly workflow instead of a required check. Find a way to run it on CI and make it required.",
    "acceptance_criteria": [
      "Root cause is documented: on the runner (elevated session, High integrity) WebView2 ignores WEBVIEW2_ADDITIONAL_BROWSER_ARGUMENTS, so no --remote-debugging-port and no DevToolsActivePort",

    "prompt": "You are working on the project \"Grain to Pixel\" in the repository at the current working directory. Follow docs/workflow.md: read this issue, post a short proposal (understanding, approach, files, tests, docs, risks, open questions) and STOP for my explicit approval before writing code. After approval implement with tests and docs, run the CI gates from docs/workflow.md section 8, open a PR that closes the GitHub issue, then report and ask for the next step.\n\nPROJECT CONTEXT (from docs/product/idea.md)\n- Grain to Pixel is a personal Windows 10/11 and macOS desktop app for close friends only, no monetization: an all-in-one tool to scan, convert, edit and organize film negatives (commercial licences are prohibitive).\n- Core workflow: ingest via Nikon Coolscan LS-50 (RGB + IR; the author's modded scanner scans whole rolls of up to 40 frames) or via separate R/G/B exposures shot with a Nikon D600 (NEF) under a Scanlight V4 light, adding tags -> determine frame area and measure film base -> convert to positive -> edit colour balance, tone, geometry, IR/ICE dust removal -> searchable tagged archive.\n- Non-goals: masking, AI generation, advanced retouching. Success: activation, and results comparable to Nikon Scan 4 / Trichroma Lab. Risk: little colour-negative sample data. Constraint: built with Claude Pro, so keep the session narrow and read only what this issue needs.\n\nTECHNICAL CONTEXT (docs/specification.md, docs/architecture.md, docs/structure.md, docs/convention.md)\n- Stack: Tauri 2; Rust workspace in crates/ (gtp-core, gtp-io, gtp-negative, gtp-edit, gtp-catalog, gtp-ingest, gtp-capture, gtp-app); React 18 + TypeScript (strict) + Vite UI in apps/desktop with Zustand, Radix UI and CSS modules; generated packages/ipc-types (tauri-specta); SQLite catalog (rusqlite bundled, FTS5, forward-only migrations); tracing logs; i18n via t() with en.json only.\n- Architecture: feature crates depend on gtp-core (gtp-ingest also on gtp-io) and never on each other; gtp-app is the application layer that combines them (services, JobManager, pipeline); src-tauri is a thin adapter (validate, call a gtp-app service, map errors to AppError); the UI never touches pixels and receives rendered bytes. Long work runs as cancellable jobs with progress events. Hardware sits behind traits (ScannerSource, LightSource, CameraSource) with fakes.\n- Pipeline (spec 4.2) fixed order: load -> IR dust -> crop -> film-base normalize -> invert -> colour -> tone -> geometry -> output transform; internal f32 linear with Rec.2020 primaries; originals immutable, all edits in the versioned Recipe.\n- Repo layout: apps/desktop (src React UI, src-tauri shell), crates/*, packages/{ipc-types,ui}, tools/{benchmark,scripts}, samples/{public,private}, docs/, decisions/ (ADRs), rules/, prompts/.\n- Conventions: no unwrap/expect/unsafe/println in committed Rust, no console.log or any in TS; validate at boundaries; AppError codes; tracing spans; tests are mandatory (unit, golden, fakes, component); Conventional Commits; performance budgets in spec section 11; security in spec section 9.\n\nISSUE T-07 – Make the Windows WebDriver e2e run on CI runners\nMilestone: M0 Foundations | Area: Tooling, CI and release | Type: test | Size: M (1d)\nSpec references: specification section 8; convention section 8\nDependencies (must be Done first; if any is not, stop and tell me): T-04 – Smoke and end-to-end tests in CI\n\nDESCRIPTION\nThe WebDriver e2e (tauri-driver + msedgedriver) passes locally but cannot start a session on GitHub-hosted Windows runners, so it is a manual/weekly workflow instead of a required check. Find a way to run it on CI and make it required.\n\nTECHNICAL DETAILS\nFILES: .github/workflows/ci.yml, .github/workflows/e2e-windows.yml, apps/desktop/tests/e2e/window.e2e.mjs, apps/desktop/src-tauri (only if the hook option is chosen).\nTESTS: the e2e run itself, green on CI. Evidence so far: runtime updated 155 to 156 and flags still ignored; runas /trustlevel does not lower integrity on the runner.\n\nACCEPTANCE CRITERIA\n- Root cause is documented: on the runner (elevated session, High integrity) WebView2 ignores WEBVIEW2_ADDITIONAL_BROWSER_ARGUMENTS, so no --remote-debugging-port and no DevToolsActivePort\n- One of these is implemented and proven: an app-side test-only debug-port hook behind a Cargo feature, a throwaway standard user for the test step, or a self-hosted or other runner\n- The e2e job passes on a pull request and is added to the required checks of develop\n- e2e-windows.yml is folded back into ci.yml (or removed) and docs/convention.md section 8 is updated\n\nNeeds from the human: approval of your proposal.\nDefinition of Done: docs/workflow.md section 11. Update the docs this change makes stale. Do not widen scope; propose follow-up issues instead.",
    "body": "## Description\nThe WebDriver e2e (tauri-driver + msedgedriver) passes locally but cannot start a session on GitHub-hosted Windows runners, so it is a manual/weekly workflow instead of a required check.   },

Colour, tone and geometry already use rayon; the candidates are the base normalize and invert stages, the downscale of the proxy on every render (`build_proxy`), the crop copy and the RGBA conversion. The two costs that mattered were the per-render downscale of the proxy and the conversion to 8-bit sRGB; normalize and invert are small. Done: the conversion looks each level up from 255 switch-over points found by bisection on the real formula (checked for every float from 0 to 1 in an ignored test, and around each threshold in the normal ones), and the downscale copies its pixels in and out on all cores. The library's own `rayon` feature was tried and dropped: it pulls in the `image` crate with its codecs (about 50 packages) and made no difference to the total. The fallback of keeping a 1600 px proxy for the open frame was not needed, so memory is unchanged.
